        Idea:
Consolidate execute paths and clean up layered machinery

## Problem
v0.9.0 added 4 features in one session (incremental saves, quality gates, batched execution, per-batch CLI) without cleaning up between layers. The result is:

### Critical bugs
1. Broken single-batch timeout recovery: prompt tells executor to write execution_checkpoint.json, but _recover_execute_timeout reads finalize.json. The checkpoint file nothing reads.
2. --batch N never blocks on incomplete tracking — always returns success=True even when tasks are missing updates.

### Structural debt  
3. Three execute paths (_handle_execute_single_batch, _handle_execute_one_batch, multi-batch loop) that duplicate ~100 lines of merge/observe logic with subtle inconsistencies: single-batch lacks git observation, different session update ordering, different blocking behavior.
4. Three overlapping recovery mechanisms (checkpoint files, _reset_timeout_invalid_tasks, per-batch persistence) that don't compose.
5. Dead code: all_completed computed but unused, stop_after_batch set after break.
6. handlers.py is 2723 lines — ~800 lines of execution logic should be its own module.

## Goal
One execute path, one recovery mechanism, no dead code, no duplication. The auto-loop should call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively rather than reimplementing the same logic. Single-batch becomes batch-1-of-1, not a separate code path. Timeout recovery reads the right checkpoint file. All paths get git observation and blocking checks.

## Approach
1. Extract execution logic into megaplan/execution.py (~800 lines out of handlers.py)
2. Create _merge_batch_results() helper for the 45-line merge block
3. Create _observe_git_changes() helper for the git observation block  
4. Eliminate _handle_execute_single_batch — single-batch goes through _handle_execute_one_batch with batch=1
5. Make the multi-batch auto-loop call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively
6. Fix timeout recovery to read execution_checkpoint.json / execution_batch_N.json
7. Add blocking checks to --batch N path
8. Remove dead code
9. Tests must still pass (310)

        Idea:
Consolidate execute paths and clean up layered machinery

## Problem
v0.9.0 added 4 features in one session (incremental saves, quality gates, batched execution, per-batch CLI) without cleaning up between layers. The result is:

### Critical bugs
1. Broken single-batch timeout recovery: prompt tells executor to write execution_checkpoint.json, but _recover_execute_timeout reads finalize.json. The checkpoint file nothing reads.
2. --batch N never blocks on incomplete tracking — always returns success=True even when tasks are missing updates.

### Structural debt  
3. Three execute paths (_handle_execute_single_batch, _handle_execute_one_batch, multi-batch loop) that duplicate ~100 lines of merge/observe logic with subtle inconsistencies: single-batch lacks git observation, different session update ordering, different blocking behavior.
4. Three overlapping recovery mechanisms (checkpoint files, _reset_timeout_invalid_tasks, per-batch persistence) that don't compose.
5. Dead code: all_completed computed but unused, stop_after_batch set after break.
6. handlers.py is 2723 lines — ~800 lines of execution logic should be its own module.

## Goal
One execute path, one recovery mechanism, no dead code, no duplication. The auto-loop should call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively rather than reimplementing the same logic. Single-batch becomes batch-1-of-1, not a separate code path. Timeout recovery reads the right checkpoint file. All paths get git observation and blocking checks.

## Approach
1. Extract execution logic into megaplan/execution.py (~800 lines out of handlers.py)
2. Create _merge_batch_results() helper for the 45-line merge block
3. Create _observe_git_changes() helper for the git observation block  
4. Eliminate _handle_execute_single_batch — single-batch goes through _handle_execute_one_batch with batch=1
5. Make the multi-batch auto-loop call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively
6. Fix timeout recovery to read execution_checkpoint.json / execution_batch_N.json
7. Add blocking checks to --batch N path
8. Remove dead code
9. Tests must still pass (310)

        Idea:
Consolidate execute paths and clean up layered machinery

## Problem
v0.9.0 added 4 features in one session (incremental saves, quality gates, batched execution, per-batch CLI) without cleaning up between layers. The result is:

### Critical bugs
1. Broken single-batch timeout recovery: prompt tells executor to write execution_checkpoint.json, but _recover_execute_timeout reads finalize.json. The checkpoint file nothing reads.
2. --batch N never blocks on incomplete tracking — always returns success=True even when tasks are missing updates.

### Structural debt  
3. Three execute paths (_handle_execute_single_batch, _handle_execute_one_batch, multi-batch loop) that duplicate ~100 lines of merge/observe logic with subtle inconsistencies: single-batch lacks git observation, different session update ordering, different blocking behavior.
4. Three overlapping recovery mechanisms (checkpoint files, _reset_timeout_invalid_tasks, per-batch persistence) that don't compose.
5. Dead code: all_completed computed but unused, stop_after_batch set after break.
6. handlers.py is 2723 lines — ~800 lines of execution logic should be its own module.

## Goal
One execute path, one recovery mechanism, no dead code, no duplication. The auto-loop should call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively rather than reimplementing the same logic. Single-batch becomes batch-1-of-1, not a separate code path. Timeout recovery reads the right checkpoint file. All paths get git observation and blocking checks.

## Approach
1. Extract execution logic into megaplan/execution.py (~800 lines out of handlers.py)
2. Create _merge_batch_results() helper for the 45-line merge block
3. Create _observe_git_changes() helper for the git observation block  
4. Eliminate _handle_execute_single_batch — single-batch goes through _handle_execute_one_batch with batch=1
5. Make the multi-batch auto-loop call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively
6. Fix timeout recovery to read execution_checkpoint.json / execution_batch_N.json
7. Add blocking checks to --batch N path
8. Remove dead code
9. Tests must still pass (310)

apply_session_update_fn(*args, **kwargs):\n    sessions.append((args, kwargs))\n\ndef append_history_fn(state, entry):\n    history.append(entry)\n\ndef make_history_entry_fn(step, **kwargs):\n    return {'step': step, **kwargs}\n\ndef save_state_fn(plan_dir, state):\n    pass\n\ndef attach_agent_fallback_fn(response, args):\n    pass\n\ndef store_raw_worker_output_fn(plan_dir, step, iteration, content):\n    path = plan_dir / f'{step}_raw.txt'\n    path.write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')\n    return path.name\n\nfor batch_number, filename in [(None, 'execution_checkpoint.json'), (1, 'execution_batch_1.json')]:\n    history.clear()\n    sessions.clear()\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        plan_dir = Path(tmp)\n        (plan_dir / 'finalize.json').write_text(json.dumps(BASE_FINALIZE), encoding='utf-8')\n        (plan_dir / filename).write_text(json.dumps(CHECKPOINT), encoding='utf-8')\n        state = {\n            'config': {'project_dir': str(plan_dir)},\n            'iteration': 1,\n            'meta': {'user_approved_gate': True},\n            'sessions': {},\n        }\n        response = _recover_execute_timeout(\n            plan_dir=plan_dir,\n            state=state,\n            error=CliError('worker_timeout', 'execute timed out', extra={'session_id': 'session-1', 'raw_output': 'partial'}),\n            agent='codex',\n            mode='persistent',\n            refreshed=False,\n            auto_approve=False,\n            args=argparse.Namespace(),\n            batch_number=batch_number,\n            apply_session_update_fn=apply_session_update_fn,\n            append_history_fn=append_history_fn,\n            make_history_entry_fn=make_history_entry_fn,\n            save_state_fn=save_state_fn,\n            attach_agent_fallback_fn=attach_agent_fallback_fn,\n            store_raw_worker_output_fn=store_raw_worker_output_fn,\n        )\n        recovered = json.loads((plan_dir / 'finalize.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n        assert recovered['tasks'][0]['status'] == 'done'\n        assert recovered['tasks'][0]['files_changed'] == ['checkpoint.txt']\n        assert recovered['sense_checks'][0]['executor_note'] == 'Recovered sense check.'\n        assert response['state'] == STATE_FINALIZED\n        assert any(filename in deviation for deviation in response['deviations'])\n        assert history[-1]['result'] == 'timeout'\n        assert sessions\n\nprint('timeout-recovery-smoke-ok')\nPY"

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

        Idea:
Consolidate execute paths and clean up layered machinery

## Problem
v0.9.0 added 4 features in one session (incremental saves, quality gates, batched execution, per-batch CLI) without cleaning up between layers. The result is:

### Critical bugs
1. Broken single-batch timeout recovery: prompt tells executor to write execution_checkpoint.json, but _recover_execute_timeout reads finalize.json. The checkpoint file nothing reads.
2. --batch N never blocks on incomplete tracking — always returns success=True even when tasks are missing updates.

### Structural debt  
3. Three execute paths (_handle_execute_single_batch, _handle_execute_one_batch, multi-batch loop) that duplicate ~100 lines of merge/observe logic with subtle inconsistencies: single-batch lacks git observation, different session update ordering, different blocking behavior.
4. Three overlapping recovery mechanisms (checkpoint files, _reset_timeout_invalid_tasks, per-batch persistence) that don't compose.
5. Dead code: all_completed computed but unused, stop_after_batch set after break.
6. handlers.py is 2723 lines — ~800 lines of execution logic should be its own module.

## Goal
One execute path, one recovery mechanism, no dead code, no duplication. The auto-loop should call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively rather than reimplementing the same logic. Single-batch becomes batch-1-of-1, not a separate code path. Timeout recovery reads the right checkpoint file. All paths get git observation and blocking checks.

## Approach
1. Extract execution logic into megaplan/execution.py (~800 lines out of handlers.py)
2. Create _merge_batch_results() helper for the 45-line merge block
3. Create _observe_git_changes() helper for the git observation block  
4. Eliminate _handle_execute_single_batch — single-batch goes through _handle_execute_one_batch with batch=1
5. Make the multi-batch auto-loop call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively
6. Fix timeout recovery to read execution_checkpoint.json / execution_batch_N.json
7. Add blocking checks to --batch N path
8. Remove dead code
9. Tests must still pass (310)

return {'step': step, **kwargs}\n\ndef save_state_fn(plan_dir, state):\n    pass\n\ndef attach_agent_fallback_fn(response, args):\n    pass\n\ndef store_raw_worker_output_fn(plan_dir, step, iteration, content):\n    path = plan_dir / f'{step}_raw.txt'\n    path.write_text(content, encoding='utf-8')\n    return path.name\n\nfor batch_number, filename in [(None, 'execution_checkpoint.json'), (1, 'execution_batch_1.json')]:\n    history.clear()\n    sessions.clear()\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        plan_dir = Path(tmp)\n        (plan_dir / 'finalize.json').write_text(json.dumps(BASE_FINALIZE), encoding='utf-8')\n        (plan_dir / filename).write_text(json.dumps(CHECKPOINT), encoding='utf-8')\n        state = {\n            'config': {'project_dir': str(plan_dir)},\n            'iteration': 1,\n            'meta': {'user_approved_gate': True},\n            'sessions': {},\n        }\n        response = _recover_execute_timeout(\n            plan_dir=plan_dir,\n            state=state,\n            error=CliError('worker_timeout', 'execute timed out', extra={'session_id': 'session-1', 'raw_output': 'partial'}),\n            agent='codex',\n            mode='persistent',\n            refreshed=False,\n            auto_approve=False,\n            args=argparse.Namespace(),\n            batch_number=batch_number,\n            apply_session_update_fn=apply_session_update_fn,\n            append_history_fn=append_history_fn,\n            make_history_entry_fn=make_history_entry_fn,\n            save_state_fn=save_state_fn,\n            attach_agent_fallback_fn=attach_agent_fallback_fn,\n            store_raw_worker_output_fn=store_raw_worker_output_fn,\n        )\n        recovered = json.loads((plan_dir / 'finalize.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))\n        assert recovered['tasks'][0]['status'] == 'done'\n        assert recovered['tasks'][0]['files_changed'] == ['checkpoint.txt']\n        assert recovered['sense_checks'][0]['executor_note'] == 'Recovered sense check.'\n        assert response['state'] == STATE_FINALIZED\n        assert any(filename in deviation for deviation in response['deviations'])\n        assert history[-1]['result'] == 'timeout'\n        assert sessions\n\nprint('timeout-recovery-smoke-ok')\nPY"

Consolidate execute paths and clean up layered machinery

## Problem
v0.9.0 added 4 features in one session (incremental saves, quality gates, batched execution, per-batch CLI) without cleaning up between layers. The result is:

### Critical bugs
1. Broken single-batch timeout recovery: prompt tells executor to write execution_checkpoint.json, but _recover_execute_timeout reads finalize.json. The checkpoint file nothing reads.
2. --batch N never blocks on incomplete tracking — always returns success=True even when tasks are missing updates.

### Structural debt  
3. Three execute paths (_handle_execute_single_batch, _handle_execute_one_batch, multi-batch loop) that duplicate ~100 lines of merge/observe logic with subtle inconsistencies: single-batch lacks git observation, different session update ordering, different blocking behavior.
4. Three overlapping recovery mechanisms (checkpoint files, _reset_timeout_invalid_tasks, per-batch persistence) that don't compose.
5. Dead code: all_completed computed but unused, stop_after_batch set after break.
6. handlers.py is 2723 lines — ~800 lines of execution logic should be its own module.

## Goal
One execute path, one recovery mechanism, no dead code, no duplication. The auto-loop should call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively rather than reimplementing the same logic. Single-batch becomes batch-1-of-1, not a separate code path. Timeout recovery reads the right checkpoint file. All paths get git observation and blocking checks.

## Approach
1. Extract execution logic into megaplan/execution.py (~800 lines out of handlers.py)
2. Create _merge_batch_results() helper for the 45-line merge block
3. Create _observe_git_changes() helper for the git observation block  
4. Eliminate _handle_execute_single_batch — single-batch goes through _handle_execute_one_batch with batch=1
5. Make the multi-batch auto-loop call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively
6. Fix timeout recovery to read execution_checkpoint.json / execution_batch_N.json
7. Add blocking checks to --batch N path
8. Remove dead code
9. Tests must still pass (310)

        Project directory:

        Idea:
Consolidate execute paths and clean up layered machinery

## Problem
v0.9.0 added 4 features in one session (incremental saves, quality gates, batched execution, per-batch CLI) without cleaning up between layers. The result is:

### Critical bugs
1. Broken single-batch timeout recovery: prompt tells executor to write execution_checkpoint.json, but _recover_execute_timeout reads finalize.json. The checkpoint file nothing reads.
2. --batch N never blocks on incomplete tracking — always returns success=True even when tasks are missing updates.

### Structural debt  
3. Three execute paths (_handle_execute_single_batch, _handle_execute_one_batch, multi-batch loop) that duplicate ~100 lines of merge/observe logic with subtle inconsistencies: single-batch lacks git observation, different session update ordering, different blocking behavior.
4. Three overlapping recovery mechanisms (checkpoint files, _reset_timeout_invalid_tasks, per-batch persistence) that don't compose.
5. Dead code: all_completed computed but unused, stop_after_batch set after break.
6. handlers.py is 2723 lines — ~800 lines of execution logic should be its own module.

## Goal
One execute path, one recovery mechanism, no dead code, no duplication. The auto-loop should call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively rather than reimplementing the same logic. Single-batch becomes batch-1-of-1, not a separate code path. Timeout recovery reads the right checkpoint file. All paths get git observation and blocking checks.

## Approach
1. Extract execution logic into megaplan/execution.py (~800 lines out of handlers.py)
2. Create _merge_batch_results() helper for the 45-line merge block
3. Create _observe_git_changes() helper for the git observation block  
4. Eliminate _handle_execute_single_batch — single-batch goes through _handle_execute_one_batch with batch=1
5. Make the multi-batch auto-loop call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively
6. Fix timeout recovery to read execution_checkpoint.json / execution_batch_N.json
7. Add blocking checks to --batch N path
8. Remove dead code
9. Tests must still pass (310)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/megaplan

        Idea:
Consolidate execute paths and clean up layered machinery

## Problem
v0.9.0 added 4 features in one session (incremental saves, quality gates, batched execution, per-batch CLI) without cleaning up between layers. The result is:

### Critical bugs
1. Broken single-batch timeout recovery: prompt tells executor to write execution_checkpoint.json, but _recover_execute_timeout reads finalize.json. The checkpoint file nothing reads.
2. --batch N never blocks on incomplete tracking — always returns success=True even when tasks are missing updates.

### Structural debt  
3. Three execute paths (_handle_execute_single_batch, _handle_execute_one_batch, multi-batch loop) that duplicate ~100 lines of merge/observe logic with subtle inconsistencies: single-batch lacks git observation, different session update ordering, different blocking behavior.
4. Three overlapping recovery mechanisms (checkpoint files, _reset_timeout_invalid_tasks, per-batch persistence) that don't compose.
5. Dead code: all_completed computed but unused, stop_after_batch set after break.
6. handlers.py is 2723 lines — ~800 lines of execution logic should be its own module.

## Goal
One execute path, one recovery mechanism, no dead code, no duplication. The auto-loop should call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively rather than reimplementing the same logic. Single-batch becomes batch-1-of-1, not a separate code path. Timeout recovery reads the right checkpoint file. All paths get git observation and blocking checks.

## Approach
1. Extract execution logic into megaplan/execution.py (~800 lines out of handlers.py)
2. Create _merge_batch_results() helper for the 45-line merge block
3. Create _observe_git_changes() helper for the git observation block  
4. Eliminate _handle_execute_single_batch — single-batch goes through _handle_execute_one_batch with batch=1
5. Make the multi-batch auto-loop call _handle_execute_one_batch iteratively
6. Fix timeout recovery to read execution_checkpoint.json / execution_batch_N.json
7. Add blocking checks to --batch N path
8. Remove dead code
9. Tests must still pass (310)
